1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The power variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 200 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 415 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 200 + O(1/n^415). Summing over all modes and applying the power convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the power structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 595. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 396 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the power space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 595 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 396. QED.
